# DvE - Italian parliament: colour of every seat. Sprite frames start at 1:
# frame 1 = empty seat, frames 2..9 = party 1..8.
# Seats are ordered left to right; party k holds the seats from its cumulative
# threshold pm_cs(k-1) up to pm_csk. Frame = 2 + number of thresholds <= seat.
ITA_parliament_seat_frames_effect = {
	set_variable = { ITA_cam_fr^0 = 2 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { pm_cs1 < 1 } } add_to_variable = { ITA_cam_fr^0 = 1 } }
